<a id="b11"></a>
## B11 — Decide hover delay and manual reveal preservation

Current capability: [R13 — 颗粒化显示、悬停、自定义评级与可访问候选](../R13_PLAN.md#display). Historical source: `11-hover-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Close the named historical non-equivalence with a retained executable behavior contract or item-specific approved disposition.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Retain which non-equivalent user behavior for Hover delay and manual reveal, or where is the item-specific approval for each non-equivalent disposition?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Java 200ms plus delay dialog/manual reveal differs from accepted Next 120ms hover. Candidate ten-second delay is unrelated. Current 120ms and non-navigating preview remain unchanged until item-specific decision. Inspect original corresponding disposition ticket and approval source already named in frozen Inventory/audits, not new Java investigation. Missing approval is not permission to delete. Decide Hover entry, adjustable/default delay, manual-reveal behavior, persistence, focus and cancellation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Source-to-user-goal table compares every named retained control/default/persistence/failure to current accepted scope; historical routing is not equivalence proof.
- Retained contract names observable entry/default/save/cancel/failure and exact successor; non-equivalent permanent change carries named approver and item-specific citation.
- Unresolved choices keep linked implementation needs-info; original Accepted item/candidate/range is preserved.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T02-H16`.

Source intents: `T02-H16`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `UI-03`.

Delta sources: Frozen baseline/preservation source; no new Delta ID invented.

Do not migrate Java 200 ms, delay setting, or manual reveal. |

## B18 — Retained user-controlled in-tree result reuse successor

Current capability: [R13 — 缓存、闪电及自动整局保留能力](../R13_PLAN.md#retained-analysis). Historical source: `18-cache-toggle-retained.md`.

`B17`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Java enable-lizzie-cache defaults true and controls computed node reuse, not Next SQLite persistence. Existing SQLite remains accepted and cannot be cited as equivalent toggle without evidence. This source contract is explicitly blocked: Cache-toggle entry/default/save, result freshness/reuse/disable semantics and distinction from SGF exchange/durable SQLite have not all been frozen. The decision must state the exact missing user behavior; no automatic reproduction of Java machinery, no new semantics assumed. If it establishes unchanged accepted equivalence for a control, close that control with exact inherited evidence rather than duplicate implementation; other retained residuals remain owned here. Scope closes only with a complete per-control decision and actual acceptance, never a silent empty feature.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Decision contract is linked and its retained entries/defaults/persistence/failure/cancel have actual acceptance; unresolved choices do not become ready.
- Failed save/cancel does not apply pending settings or start/switch an engine; old work cannot mutate current game/Run.
- Every original non-equivalent goal is delivered or individually approved and evidenced; no speculative broad Accepted promotion.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Scope closes only with a complete per-control decision and actual acceptance, never a silent empty feature.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

## B23 — Decide tracking job historical preservation preservation

Current capability: [R13 — Tracking 区间、目标与清除](../R13_PLAN.md#tracking). Historical source: `23-tracking-cut-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Close the named historical non-equivalence with a retained executable behavior contract or item-specific approved disposition.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Retain which non-equivalent user behavior for Tracking job historical preservation, or where is the item-specific approval for each non-equivalent disposition?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Tracking point set/interval/target visits/display identity/clear-to-ordinary goals are not hover eviction or readboard. Java tracking defaults max visits500 and has interval controls; same-tree focus is later evolution and must not silently delete legacy interval goal. Inspect original corresponding disposition ticket and approval source already named in frozen Inventory/audits, not new Java investigation. Missing approval is not permission to delete. Decide Exact retained tracking goals versus old exclusion and later same-tree focus, with any non-equivalent remainder individually approved.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Source-to-user-goal table compares every named retained control/default/persistence/failure to current accepted scope; historical routing is not equivalence proof.
- Retained contract names observable entry/default/save/cancel/failure and exact successor; non-equivalent permanent change carries named approver and item-specific citation.
- Unresolved choices keep linked implementation needs-info; original Accepted item/candidate/range is preserved.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B26 — Freeze batch intake, analysis conditions and safe file output

Current capability: [R13 — 多文件分析队列与安全输出](../R13_PLAN.md#batch). Historical source: `26-batch-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Freeze missing batch settings/output choices without changing the session-only ordered queue requirement.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: What analysis/output/default/queue-edit and stop-current/all contract is admitted for session-only batch SGF analysis?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Consume ANA02/03 and APP02 multi-file intake plus source batch/batch-deep menus/table/default100 visits and PR529 startup/admission. Decide supported input/output SGF paths, per-file analysis scope/stages/budgets/defaults, stop-current versus stop-all and edit-queue rules, output overwrite/atomic preservation and explicit admission while foreground owner is occupied. No restoring queue/history from restart. Batch work must never replace/dirty current game.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- All actual menus/toolbar/table/multi-file drop share intake contract with order, duplicates/invalid-file handling and per-file states.
- Analysis settings/defaults/persistence and output action/path/overwrite/failed-save rules are named, source-backed and non-destructive.
- Waiting admission remains visible; Cancel current/all have distinct terminal and stale file/Run/job identity rules.
- APP02 admission is a consumer seam, not installed activation/Release dependency; exact blocked B27 behavior is listed.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B27 — Session-only ordered SGF batch analysis

Current capability: [R13 — 多文件分析队列与安全输出](../R13_PLAN.md#batch). Historical source: `27-batch.md`.

### Capability result

Implement explicit batch/batch-deep intake and safe per-file execution without replacing the current game.

### Functional input scope

`B26`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Menu/toolbar/table and APP02 multi-file drop feed the same ordered session queue; explicit settings/confirmation uses B26 conditions. Per-file pending/running/completed/failed/cancelled and waiting admission never disappear. Worker owns frozen input file/Run/job identities, publishes only current batch identity, and honours admitted current/all cancellation. Explicit output uses source snapshot/approved safe output contract; failed output preserves original file. Failed/cancelled setting save cannot start jobs. Queue is never persisted/restored, current game/tree/cursor/dirty flag are untouched. No extra process merely to bypass foreground admission.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Actual intake surfaces route one queue with visible order/admission/state and explicit stop-current/stop-all results.
- Late completion after new batch/file/cancel/Run cannot update a different batch or current game.
- Malformed/unavailable file, incompatible engine, cancellation and output failure preserve current game and original input files.
- Restart restores no unfinished queue or live job; actual file/qualified-engine evidence records limits.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B28 — Admit named engine/version rich-analysis adapters

Current capability: [R13 — 具名丰富分析适配与缺字段显示](../R13_PLAN.md#adapters). Historical source: `28-adapter-evidence.md`.

### Capability result

Produce named product engine/version admission and controlled protocol fixtures before an adapter feature is executable.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Which named engine/version rich-analysis capabilities and protocol samples are actually admitted?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Identify each requested engine/version and exact supported candidates/winrate/PV/ownership/streaming/selected-node/whole-game fields. Record raw protocol samples, valid/absent/error/terminal/cancel and identity behavior. Each capability is independent; missing fields unavailable, not invented. Decide precise admitted set and unsupported outcomes; no empty generic interface or automatic engine build. Future SSH adapters obtain additional compatibility/service evidence under C.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Named engine/version/source/binary compatibility product list is approved, with protocol fixtures per admitted capability.
- Missing fields/unsupported commands/protocol failure/late output have observable expected mappings and honest unavailable states.
- Feature B29 is blocked until this concrete result; no empty adapter interface counts as delivery.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

These acceptance cases are obligations, not passed checks. Existing original evidence is inherited only if unchanged.


<a id="b30"></a>
## B30 — Freeze automatic-on-load quick-analysis admission and saved defaults

Current capability: [R13 — 自动载入快析与任务交还](../R13_PLAN.md#autoquick). Historical source: `30-quick-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Freeze actual load trigger/default/settings/ownership for automatic quick analysis, not just an explicit ANA16 task.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Which successful-load triggers, default/persistence and single-Run handback policy are admitted for automatic quick analysis?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Input source auto-quick-analyze-on-load default true, analysis-max-visits default1, flash controls and current SGF07 transaction/ENG06/ANA16 ownership. Decide Next first-use default/persistence and supported trigger surfaces, late engine-ready admission, task conditions, user browsing/pause/Cancel and safe handback. No hidden start/switch/fallback after failed load or no engine. Retain separate explicit tasks and remote lifecycle extensions; source process topology is not imported.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Successful independent load, failed/cancelled replacement, no-engine and late-ready behavior each have explicit trigger/default/save/ownership results.
- Pause/cancel/navigation/close/new load/engine or rules change retire or preserve intent as specified without cursor rollback.
- Local execution mode and failure/handback/Continue gate are executable, remote-only admission remains C-specific.
- Only precise new automatic paths are blocked; current lifecycle/framing/local-Restart investigations stay independent.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK`.

Source intents: `T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK`, `T03-AUTOLOAD-PAUSE-BOUNDARIES`, `T03-ANA16-EXECUTION-MODE-INDICATOR`, `T05-AUTO-QUICK-OWNER`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ANA-19`, `ANA-16`, `ENG-06`.

Delta sources: `UD-03-022`, `UD-03-051`, `UD-05-17`, `UD-05-25`.

#### Baseline entries, defaults and persistence

##### CAP-04-ANA-06

| `CAP-04-ANA-06` | Fast overview analysis of game / part / all branches + settings | `FlashAnalyzeMenu` whole/part/all-branches/settings; `AutoAnalyzeMenu` lightning; Ctrl+B; right-click flash-this-move; silent load-flash settings are this Capability, not a new row | `analysis-max-visits` default 1. `auto-quick-analyze-on-load` default true. Toolbar `flash-analyze` defaults false when double menu + top toolbar, else true | Missing command → settings dialog; failed settings save does not start analysis. Cancel via `flashMode` / `tryStopFlashModeForManualGame`. | Source: `FlashAnalyzeMenu.java:11-46`; `AutoAnalyzeMenu.java:75-84`; `Config.java:1900-1901,1830-1832`; `RightClickMenu.java:185-191` | Whether load-flash runs on a file without a catalog engine (default) | Successor restoration (Q17–Q24) | `ANA-16` explicit quick overview of the current game, interval, selected line or all branches, with preview and task budgets. Automatic quick analysis on file load remains a separate restoration obligation; neither `ANA-01` nor `ANA-02` absorbs it. |

###### UD-03-022 — Pause、当前任务取消、Resume 与新载入边界
- **核心文件出处**: `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/analysis/AnalysisEngine.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/analysis/Leelaz.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/gui/BottomToolbar.java`
- **用户可见行为与边界**: Pause取消当前自动棋谱任务；专用worker结束、共享借用恢复且不退出进程；Resume仅当前局面；较晚的新kifu load可开始新自动任务。旧restore不得绕过暂停。
- **对等项 / 任务映射**: ANA-06 / ANA-16（Accepted，原范围）；T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK；`T03-AUTOLOAD-PAUSE-BOUNDARIES`。所有历史项仅引用Matrix原范围，最终后继ID由06分配。
- **Tauri 现状 / 证据与残余**: 已有连续暂停/显式有限任务不等于自动载入快析；保留source新载入边界，不新增永久暂停策略。
- **处置结论**: 需后继功能/修复。任务 kind=`feature`；不把Source差异等同Next缺陷。
- **功能断言 / 调查停止条件**: 当前自动任务取消后其迟到完成不启动ponder；Resume只分析当前局面；下一次独立有效载入按自动快析设置准入，等待旧cleanup；不退出健康共享Run、不影响独立lane。
- **实际阻塞与未来证据门**: T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK的准入/交接合同；源d7edb329 patch:11–19,30–32。
- **唯一批次建议 / 责任**: B；`T03-AUTOLOAD-PAUSE-BOUNDARIES` 拥有此Delta的结果。具体合同按来源保留，同源内容去重不改变结果责任。

- 用户行为/边界：本组包含两条不同责任链。本地：自动快析交还失败后导入另一棋谱，用户显式 Restart 当前本地引擎，在新 reader/generation 重新确认该棋谱的规则和位置后才允许 Space 继续；保持暂停/失败门禁，旧局面、旧规则许可、旧回调不能解锁新局面。远程：remote-only 快析/停止响应 reader lease、断线重连、曲线和外部同步交还；不能因同组提交而混同本地恢复。
- Tauri映射/具名任务意图：本地 T05-LOCAL-IMPORT-RESTART-CHECK（B），受影响 ENG-02（Restart/Run）、ANA-16（任务）、ANA-06（持续分析）、SGF-01/SGF-07（导入/替换）；远程 T05-REMOTE-QUICK-HANDBACK（C），依赖本地所有权契约及 RCOMP-01，涉及 GAME-10 时另消费其确认契约。ANA-11不拥有自动快析。
- 覆盖证据或缺口：PR591的 `f360decb0440abfa82303ad9fe69cdd67a1070c5` 明确仅本地显式重启（`!targetEngine.useRemoteCompute`），不改变远程重连；`90afca6be97cd52245f815b1f1ffa89c137021e2`、`5b6a1333da8ec06e0fb219b70fbfd9e67bd9bda6` 保留比较退出/导入恢复交接。固定 Tauri lifecycle.rs:644–696 已在 Restart 退休旧任务并建立新 Run，document_departure.rs:418–490 拥有本地替换；这是调查起点而非新原生证据或已证缺陷。远程 PR584/587/592/595/596 中 stop response 先核对 reader binding；不复制 Java remoteGTP。
- 处置：本地已支持导入/显式 Restart/继续路径先做有界调查，不等 RCOMP 或远程账号；自动打开快析的新增部分与 T05-AUTO-QUICK-OWNER 串行整合。不得自动重启、自动恢复暂停意图或仅凭 Ready 清除局面确认。远程后继等本地任务所有权和远程协议稳定后接入，无真实服务证据不Passed。
- 后续验收/调查停止条件：本地受支持引擎验证任务交还失败→换谱→用户显式 Restart→新规则/位置确认→用户继续，以及失败、取消、又换谱/规则/引擎、迟到旧回调；新谱/Run/暂停意图不被旧结果污染，无问题记录所测边界，有问题才修复。远程另验真实端点停止/断线/重连、排队重启/取消与外部同步：无旧曲线/旧落子、不隐藏失败，不以loopback代替真实服务。

### Evidence, native gaps and invalidation

No product/native run is required to decide policy. Source/product approval and a decision-complete output are the acceptance surface. Native/engine/protocol evidence needed for implementation is assigned to the blocked feature; no fabricated live result.

<a id="b31"></a>
## B31 — Automatic load quick analysis and identity-safe handback

Current capability: [R13 — 自动载入快析与任务交还](../R13_PLAN.md#autoquick). Historical source: `31-quick.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver successful-load automatic quick analysis with safe pause, cancellation, visible mode and user-position preservation.

### Functional input scope

`B30`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Apply approved successful-load settings only after SGF07 commit and Run/rules/position admission; never implicitly start/switch/fallback. Delayed engine-ready or task completion cannot pull browsing back to old node, publish old curve or override pause. Cancel current automatic task fences late completion/ponder; Resume only current position. New independent successful load follows saved quick setting after old cleanup, without killing healthy shared Run or independent lane. Display actual admitted execution target/mode, not requested option; local display does not wait remote credentials. Completion/failure/cancel/close handback is single and identity-bound; explicit Restart must reconfirm new local rules/position before user Continue when recovery failed. C owns remote-only lifecycle/service and mode integration.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Every successful-load entry/default/save/failure/cancel outcome matches B30; explicit ANA16 does not stand in for automatic trigger.
- Late start/old completion/browsing/new SGF/engine/rules/Pause/Cancel/close keep new node/Run/paused intent and current curve correct.
- Cancel leaves healthy Run/other lane; Resume current target and later valid load respects setting after old cleanup.
- Mode label reflects actual admitted local/shared task; remote unavailable is explicit and does not block local display.
- Compatible real local engine demonstrates full curve and single handback; local failed-handback→import→explicit Restart→confirm→Continue chain is recorded independently of remote evidence.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK`.

Source intents: `T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK`, `T03-AUTOLOAD-PAUSE-BOUNDARIES`, `T03-ANA16-EXECUTION-MODE-INDICATOR`, `T05-AUTO-QUICK-OWNER`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ANA-19`, `ANA-16`, `ENG-06`.

Delta sources: `UD-03-022`, `UD-03-051`, `UD-05-17`, `UD-05-25`.

Toolbar `flash-analyze` defaults false when double menu + top toolbar, else true | Missing command → settings dialog; failed settings save does not start analysis. Cancel via `flashMode` / `tryStopFlashModeForManualGame`. | Source: `FlashAnalyzeMenu.java:11-46`; `AutoAnalyzeMenu.java:75-84`; `Config.java:1900-1901,1830-1832`; `RightClickMenu.java:185-191` | Whether load-flash runs on a file without a catalog engine (default) | Successor restoration (Q17–Q24) | `ANA-16` explicit quick overview of the current game, interval, selected line or all branches, with preview and task budgets. Automatic quick analysis on file load remains a separate restoration obligation; neither `ANA-01` nor `ANA-02` absorbs it. |

###### UD-03-022 — Pause、当前任务取消、Resume 与新载入边界
- **核心文件出处**: `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/analysis/AnalysisEngine.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/analysis/Leelaz.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/gui/BottomToolbar.java`
- **用户可见行为与边界**: Pause取消当前自动棋谱任务；专用worker结束、共享借用恢复且不退出进程；Resume仅当前局面；较晚的新kifu load可开始新自动任务。旧restore不得绕过暂停。
- **对等项 / 任务映射**: ANA-06 / ANA-16（Accepted，原范围）；T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK；`T03-AUTOLOAD-PAUSE-BOUNDARIES`。所有历史项仅引用Matrix原范围，最终后继ID由06分配。
- **Tauri 现状 / 证据与残余**: 已有连续暂停/显式有限任务不等于自动载入快析；保留source新载入边界，不新增永久暂停策略。
- **处置结论**: 需后继功能/修复。任务 kind=`feature`；不把Source差异等同Next缺陷。
- **功能断言 / 调查停止条件**: 当前自动任务取消后其迟到完成不启动ponder；Resume只分析当前局面；下一次独立有效载入按自动快析设置准入，等待旧cleanup；不退出健康共享Run、不影响独立lane。
- **实际阻塞与未来证据门**: T02-AUTOLOAD-QUICK的准入/交接合同；源d7edb329 patch:11–19,30–32。
- **唯一批次建议 / 责任**: B；`T03-AUTOLOAD-PAUSE-BOUNDARIES` 拥有此Delta的结果。具体合同按来源保留，同源内容去重不改变结果责任。

- 用户行为/边界：本组包含两条不同责任链。本地：自动快析交还失败后导入另一棋谱，用户显式 Restart 当前本地引擎，在新 reader/generation 重新确认该棋谱的规则和位置后才允许 Space 继续；保持暂停/失败门禁，旧局面、旧规则许可、旧回调不能解锁新局面。远程：remote-only 快析/停止响应 reader lease、断线重连、曲线和外部同步交还；不能因同组提交而混同本地恢复。
- Tauri映射/具名任务意图：本地 T05-LOCAL-IMPORT-RESTART-CHECK（B），受影响 ENG-02（Restart/Run）、ANA-16（任务）、ANA-06（持续分析）、SGF-01/SGF-07（导入/替换）；远程 T05-REMOTE-QUICK-HANDBACK（C），依赖本地所有权契约及 RCOMP-01，涉及 GAME-10 时另消费其确认契约。ANA-11不拥有自动快析。
- 覆盖证据或缺口：PR591的 `f360decb0440abfa82303ad9fe69cdd67a1070c5` 明确仅本地显式重启（`!targetEngine.useRemoteCompute`），不改变远程重连；`90afca6be97cd52245f815b1f1ffa89c137021e2`、`5b6a1333da8ec06e0fb219b70fbfd9e67bd9bda6` 保留比较退出/导入恢复交接。固定 Tauri lifecycle.rs:644–696 已在 Restart 退休旧任务并建立新 Run，document_departure.rs:418–490 拥有本地替换；这是调查起点而非新原生证据或已证缺陷。远程 PR584/587/592/595/596 中 stop response 先核对 reader binding；不复制 Java remoteGTP。
- 处置：本地已支持导入/显式 Restart/继续路径先做有界调查，不等 RCOMP 或远程账号；自动打开快析的新增部分与 T05-AUTO-QUICK-OWNER 串行整合。不得自动重启、自动恢复暂停意图或仅凭 Ready 清除局面确认。远程后继等本地任务所有权和远程协议稳定后接入，无真实服务证据不Passed。
- 后续验收/调查停止条件：本地受支持引擎验证任务交还失败→换谱→用户显式 Restart→新规则/位置确认→用户继续，以及失败、取消、又换谱/规则/引擎、迟到旧回调；新谱/Run/暂停意图不被旧结果污染，无问题记录所测边界，有问题才修复。远程另验真实端点停止/断线/重连、排队重启/取消与外部同步：无旧曲线/旧落子、不隐藏失败，不以loopback代替真实服务。

### Evidence, native gaps and invalidation

Renderer-only features require actual supported input/layout native proof, not unrelated engine execution. A browser/fixture is not a Windows/native/real-engine/service pass.

`B32`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### Guardrails and non-goals

No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

## B34 — Locate and decide custom review-grade thresholds

Current capability: [R13 — 颗粒化显示、悬停、自定义评级与可访问候选](../R13_PLAN.md#display). Historical source: `34-grade-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Resolve a source-backed custom-grade contract instead of claiming Java already has an unverified implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: What source/product-approved custom threshold semantics are intended, including equality boundaries and missing analysis?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Bounded question: frozen SET-NEXT-MOVE/SET-WINRATE-GRAPH custom grade entry/threshold/default/save/consumer versus existing Next fixed six-grade behavior. Record exact source or source-not-found without deleting spec user goal. Decide approved units/threshold order/equality/boundaries/missing-data/common perspective and atomic persistence. Do not fabricate thresholds or replace accepted fixed grades before approval.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Named source and entry/keys/defaults/consumer or explicit source-not-found result are delivered.
- Approved custom threshold boundary/unit/order/missing-data/save/cancel contract is executable and distinguished from fixed six grades.
- Any non-equivalent goal deletion has item-specific approval; B35 stays needs-info until result.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

<a id="b36"></a>
## B36 — Decide leaf-only and every-beat Engine Continuation authority

Current capability: [R13 — 两类引擎续走与唯一落子权威](../R13_PLAN.md#continuation). Historical source: `36-continuation-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver approved user semantics and writing authority for both Engine Continuation goals.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: What approved move-creation semantics and Match/current-game authority apply independently to leaf-only and every-beat continuation?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Use SGF03ADJAUTOPLAY/Ticket28 and glossary. Name leaf-only and every-beat creation semantics separately. Freeze entries/default/settings, selected-branch behavior, one Run/job scheduling, Match/current-game write authority, stop/cancel/failure/illegal decision/save boundaries and conflicts with review navigation. Engine Continuation creates moves; Review Autoplay traverses existing moves and Variation Replay only previews. Neither substitutes for continuation. No inherited historical exclusion authorizes deletion.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Both goals have explicit writing/admission/identity and stop/failure semantics plus approval/source.
- One authoritative Match/current-game owner and Run schedule prevent competing writes or implicit switches.
- Per-goal entry/default/save/cancel/illegal/stale-result behavior unlocks feature; if permanent cut requested each has approval.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

<a id="b38"></a>
## B38 — Isolated saved-profile custom benchmark runner

Current capability: [R13 — 独立 Benchmark、保存建议与实测调优](../R13_PLAN.md#performance). Historical source: `38-benchmark-runner.md`.

### Capability result

Run explicit local custom benchmarks for a frozen existing saved-profile target without changing tuning policy.

### Functional input scope

`B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Use an explicit Benchmark action on selected saved local target; default idle, session-only execution. Capture stable ENG01 profile ID/revision, original complete argv, every config layer, workdir and qualified binary/model/config identity at admission. Only compatible direct-local benchmark capability is admitted; remote/javaSSH/externalSSH is visibly unsupported here and does not block local runner. Start/stream/status/exit code/failure/Cancel are owned by one benchmark slot, not foreground GTP or policy. Cancel/close retires only this slot and bounds process cleanup/output using actual admitted runner limits; never mixes output with foreground Run. Return immutable target-ID/input-revision result; no implicit Save/recommendation/config rewrite. Runner does not wait for saved-thread policy or dynamic runtime threads.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- A saved custom target executes original argv/config layers/workdir with success/failure/exit code and exact identity visible.
- Cancel/failure/new benchmark/deleted or edited target fences old output/result and affects only benchmark slot; foreground GTP and current game remain correct.
- No benchmark Start/finish implicitly saves recommendation or mutates policy/config; remote unsupported does not inject local thread settings.
- Actual qualified compatible binary proves local runner; output/process bounds and missing metrics are recorded without engine compilation/Release requirement.

## B39 — Distinct NN and search-speed benchmark results

Current capability: [R13 — 独立 Benchmark、保存建议与实测调优](../R13_PLAN.md#performance). Historical source: `39-benchmark-metrics.md`.

### Capability result

Present two truthful benchmark metrics for the same frozen saved target.

### Functional input scope

`B38`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Consume runner result target/input revision: NN eval/s and visits/playouts/s have separate labels, units and backend/model/thread identity. Never derive one from the other or fabricate absent metrics. Session result displays success/failure/unknown for the captured target, not later edited profile. This metric-only presentation consumes runner target ownership; it does not wait for implementation of recommendation persistence. Recommendation integration belongs saved-thread-policy.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Same benchmark binds both independent labels/units to captured target and effective measured threads.
- Missing/unsupported/failed metric remains unknown with terminal state; no aliasing NN speed to search speed.
- Profile/revision changes and stale runner output cannot relabel a previous result as current recommendation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B40 — Accessible benchmark outcomes and recommendation distinction

Current capability: [R13 — 独立 Benchmark、保存建议与实测调优](../R13_PLAN.md#performance). Historical source: `40-benchmark-a11y.md`.

### Capability result

Integrate truthful benchmark/recommendation feedback into actual keyboard and assistive-technology workflow.

### Functional input scope

`B39`, `B41`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Result running/completed/failed/cancelled and hardware target have accessible text; never rely solely on color. Recommended, saved policy, effective launch and actual runtime values are distinct. Preserved focus and screen-reader outcome follow current task identity; stale completion cannot announce a new target outcome. This integration consumes recommendation-policy result but does not gate standalone metric display or manual runtime controls. F owns complete locales/cross-functional accessibility closeout, not initial usability.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Keyboard/screen-reader can identify actual outcome, separate speed metrics and recommended versus effective/actual threads without color.
- Focus remains usable on completion/failure/cancel and stale announcements are fenced.
- Unknown/missing result or unsupported target is readable and not mistaken for applied tuning.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T03-PERFORMANCE-A11Y-BENCHMARK`.

Source intents: `T03-PERFORMANCE-A11Y-BENCHMARK`.

###### UD-03-017 — benchmark 结果与线程推荐无障碍反馈
- **核心文件出处**: `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/gui/AccessibilitySupport.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/gui/KataGoAutoSetupDialog.java`
- **用户可见行为与边界**: 结果、推荐线程、硬件类型和失败状态可被读屏/键盘理解；不要求复制Swing AccessibleDescription或指定HTML标签形状。

<a id="b41"></a>
## B41 — Saved-entry CFG/BENCHMARK recommendation policy and launch integration

Current capability: [R13 — 独立 Benchmark、保存建议与实测调优](../R13_PLAN.md#performance). Historical source: `41-saved-thread-policy.md`.

### Capability result

Persist and consume per-entry measured thread recommendations without global contamination or rewriting user commands.

### Functional input scope

`B38`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Existing stable saved-entry ID owns CFG/BENCHMARK policy, recommendation and sourceRevision; undecided defaults CFG. Save alone commits, Cancel/failed save keeps durable value. CFG never injects recommendation; BENCHMARK requires valid result from isolated runner for same target/input revision. Explicit effective override wins; preserve user config bytes, command layers and workdir. Changed/unknown engine/model/config/command/workdir/environment is visible and invalidates recommendation; deleted/stale target cannot receive result. Two entries remain independent. Remote/javaSSH/externalSSH never local-tune. Manual runtime Apply/readback is not this feature and never depends on benchmark. This direction is runner→recommendation-policy only.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Two saved entries benchmark/save/reload keep separate IDs/revisions/policy and recommendations.
- CFG and explicit overrides remain unchanged; BENCHMARK is unavailable without a matching valid result and applies only at the matching entry launch.
- Cancel/failed save/stale result/deleted or changed entry/environment unknown do not mutate durable policy or wrong target.
- No saved command/config bytes rewritten, no global recommendation, no local benchmark/thread injection for remote targets.
- Actual launch evidence confirms effective selected-entry value independently of static argv historical and dynamic runtime claims.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B45 — Design compatible measured-scene overlays and report admission

Current capability: [R13 — 独立 Benchmark、保存建议与实测调优](../R13_PLAN.md#performance). Historical source: `45-measured-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Translate the fixed measured-report schema to admitted Next scene/settings/one-Run boundaries before feature implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Technical output: compatible fixed version1 schema mapping, live/whole-game settings/launch overlays and saved-local-command resolution integrated with one-Run/lane worker identity and busy/generation ownership.

This is executor-owned source/sample and technical design work within the frozen behavior, not unresolved product approval. Only a specifically evidenced unsupported case, proposed non-equivalent cut or source/ADR conflict needs an item-specific disposition; it does not block unaffected behavior.

### User behavior and boundaries

Preserve every version1 measurement and fingerprint boundary quoted below; map the already frozen live/whole-game scenes, report schema/allowed keys, saved local engine resolution, ordered recursive includes and scene overlay persistence to Next with a named busy/generation owner. Report data never executes paths/commandSemantics. Define engine/app measurement surface, actual worker-exit Cancel, thresholds from fixed source schema and treatment of missing RAM evidence as experimental only. These are executor-owned compatibility/design results, not new product choices. Do not invent new thresholds, rounds, unsupported platforms or unconditional recommendation. Startup evaluation policy B47 is separate; manual controls/isolated runner do not wait for this.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Complete report schema/version/scene allowed keys and exact fixed source numeric threshold units are attached, not only sample values or threads argv.
- Scene/overlay/Restore/commands include byte preservation, live versus whole-game and unsupported SSH/reuse rules.
- Fingerprint, background verification, actual measurement proof, RAM-regression experimental status, busy/close/hide/stale behavior are executable.
- Implementation and stage acceptance outputs distinguish controlled fixtures, engine-only/app/native/hardware and not-run gaps.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T04-MEASURED-TUNING`.

Source intents: `T04-MEASURED-TUNING`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-15`, `ENG-12`, `ENG-14`.

Delta sources: `UD-04-042`, `UD-04-047`, `UD-04-048`.

#### Frozen source behavior contracts

Each source retains its complete original requirement below. This source contract delivers its bounded technical or capability-evidence result; siblings listed by the batch route own other parts of a shared source. Source feature assertions remain mandatory for the successor, not implementation authority during this investigation.

##### T04-MEASURED-TUNING

唯一owner `T04-MEASURED-TUNING`；B；新scope未分配Parity；不重定义ENG-06；T04-RESOURCE/模型identity只消费所需部分。；性质 具名measured-report用户功能后继。

- **kind**: 具名measured-report用户功能后继
- **batch / owner**: B；新scope未分配Parity；不重定义ENG-06；T04-RESOURCE/模型identity只消费所需部分。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-042、UD-04-047、UD-04-048。
- **actual blockers**: 先冻结Next scene overlays/settings与已有单Run/lane身份兼容设计和schema准入；3/5 rounds等以已冻结source合同为依据，不在本审计跑benchmark。静态argv不满足该前置。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
1. scene为live或whole-game，report import→后台验证→review展示saved engine identity、参数/paired speed gain→独立explicit Apply；只review/import不改变settings。resolve engine/model/config只能从saved local command，不执行report paths/commandSemantics。verify engine/model/config SHA256、ordered recursively included configs、GPU/hardware/driver/memory及有效non-tuning command settings；不忽略visit/time/precision/PV/search参数。include不可用/unsupported是failure，同尺寸/时间替换也必须失效。
2. live参数numSearchThreads/nnMaxBatchSize；whole-game参数numAnalysisThreads/numSearchThreadsPerAnalysisThread/nnMaxBatchSize。接受的scene launch overlay可以优先该scene手动参数，但保存原command原字节/明确overrides不被rewrite，下次对应engine启动生效；Restore移除两个accepted overlay且保留后续manual command edits及legacy Apple tuning key。whole-game overlay不spill quick/HumanSL；separate SSH whole-game或analysisReuseCurrentEngine在review/apply/launch均unsupported，slow hash后重验，不禁local live。
3. asset/hardware/driver/command变化使recommendation失效并保留defaults；setup discovery/model change/benchmark/import/restore共享busy gate，连已queued动作和late callbacks也不能绕过。close/hide取消worker、invalidate generation；reopen后old success/error/confirmation不得显示或Apply。current saved engine identity不被later discovery retarget。model hashing不在render/event loop，opt-in verify在review/apply/launch，cold verification成本分记。
4. version1的measurement limits保持：engine-only输出不能替app固定budget completion/response/event-loop；whole-game rootVisitsByTurn每position达到budget，Cancel metric是active worker实际process exit，不是JSON termination ACK。3或5独立warm pairs，cold不进runs，有限metrics/scene allowed keys/unsupported schema拒绝；paired speed每pair更快且median至少1.03；三轮CV>10%须五轮，五轮CV>15%拒绝；latency median/max、memory余量阈值按固定schema。sampled total GPU memory不是continuous peak/per-process VRAM，也不证明RAM/heap/RSS无回归；未决RAM regression只能experimental，保留原config，不给unconditional recommendation。
- **验证环境/责任**: source schema/状态/设置fault fixtures；后续真实controlled machine/driver/workload/one-KataGo measurement与native review/apply/restore，样例数值不算证据。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 完整workflow/identity/nonmutation/busy/stale/measurement boundaries均有结果；不能仅threads argv通过。

<a id="b46"></a>
## B46 — Measured report Import, Review, Apply and Restore

Current capability: [R13 — 独立 Benchmark、保存建议与实测调优](../R13_PLAN.md#performance). Historical source: `46-measured-tuning.md`.

### Capability result

Implement complete measured-report review and scene-specific explicit launch overlay workflow, not merely thread argv.

### Functional input scope

`B45`, `B38`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

Consume B45 compatible fixed-schema/scene-overlay design; B38 only supplies the isolated worker slot with start/exit/Cancel, frozen saved-profile target/input-revision identity, and retirement/busy/generation ownership needed by setup/model/benchmark/import/restore. The full custom benchmark UI/results and B41 CFG/BENCHMARK recommendation save/launch policy are not start blockers. B46 resolves the next matching engine/scene from the existing saved local-command boundary and owns its overlay integration/revalidation. B01 qualification and A validated locale/resource-key/fallback remain scoped inputs, not unresolved product approval.

### User behavior and boundaries

Import→background validation→Review uses current saved local command only to resolve engine/model/config; report paths/commandSemantics never execute. Review displays saved identity, parameters and paired gain and never changes settings; Apply is separate explicit confirmation. Revalidate SHA256 executable/model/ordered recursive config includes, GPU/hardware/driver/memory and effective non-tuning command fingerprint (visit/time/precision/PV/search included), including after slow hash at Review/Apply/Launch. Unavailable/unsupported include or same-size/time replacement invalidates. Live overlay: numSearchThreads/nnMaxBatchSize; whole-game: numAnalysisThreads/numSearchThreadsPerAnalysisThread/nnMaxBatchSize. Preserve original command bytes and explicit overrides, apply at next matching scene launch; Restore removes both accepted overlays while retaining later manual command edits and legacy Apple key. Whole-game never spills quick/HumanSL; separate SSH whole-game or analysisReuseCurrentEngine unsupported at review/apply/launch, but local live remains allowed. Shared setup/model/benchmark/import/restore busy gate includes queued actions; close/hide cancels worker/invalidates generation and old callbacks or confirmations remain invalid after reopen. Hashing never blocks render loop. Every fixed version1 measurement rule and qualified memory claim in source contract below is mandatory, not an example.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Nonmutating Import/Review and separate Apply/Restore preserve saved engine identity/command bytes/manual edits and scene boundaries.
- All fingerprint/recursive include/effective command/hardware/version invalidations block recommendation and retain defaults at Review/Apply/Launch, including slow-hash race.
- Busy queued/late callbacks and close/hide/reopen cannot reopen/Apply stale review; current saved engine is never discovery-retargeted.
- Engine-only metrics never substitute app fixed-budget/response/event-loop results; whole-game rootVisitsByTurn meets per-position budgets and Cancel uses actual worker process exit.
- Fixed 3/5 warm-pair, speed/CV/latency/memory/schema limits from B45 are enforced; cold excluded from runs and verification cost separate.
- Sampled total GPU memory is qualified, not peak/per-process/RAM proof; unresolved RAM regression yields experimental only and preserves original config.
- Actual controlled machine/driver/workload/compatible-engine/native review/apply/restore results and absent environments are recorded; no invented benchmark pass.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T04-MEASURED-TUNING`.

Source intents: `T04-MEASURED-TUNING`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-15`, `ENG-12`, `ENG-14`.

Delta sources: `UD-04-042`, `UD-04-047`, `UD-04-048`.

#### Frozen source behavior contracts

Each source retains its complete original requirement below. This source covers only its stated scope/decision; current capability routes own other parts of a shared source. Source feature assertions accompanying a decision are requirements for its successor, not commands to implement during the investigation.

##### T04-MEASURED-TUNING

唯一owner `T04-MEASURED-TUNING`；B；新scope未分配Parity；不重定义ENG-06；T04-RESOURCE/模型identity只消费所需部分。；性质 具名measured-report用户功能后继。

- **kind**: 具名measured-report用户功能后继
- **batch / owner**: B；新scope未分配Parity；不重定义ENG-06；T04-RESOURCE/模型identity只消费所需部分。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-042、UD-04-047、UD-04-048。
- **actual blockers**: 先冻结Next scene overlays/settings与已有单Run/lane身份兼容设计和schema准入；3/5 rounds等以已冻结source合同为依据，不在本审计跑benchmark。静态argv不满足该前置。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
1. scene为live或whole-game，report import→后台验证→review展示saved engine identity、参数/paired speed gain→独立explicit Apply；只review/import不改变settings。resolve engine/model/config只能从saved local command，不执行report paths/commandSemantics。verify engine/model/config SHA256、ordered recursively included configs、GPU/hardware/driver/memory及有效non-tuning command settings；不忽略visit/time/precision/PV/search参数。include不可用/unsupported是failure，同尺寸/时间替换也必须失效。
2. live参数numSearchThreads/nnMaxBatchSize；whole-game参数numAnalysisThreads/numSearchThreadsPerAnalysisThread/nnMaxBatchSize。接受的scene launch overlay可以优先该scene手动参数，但保存原command原字节/明确overrides不被rewrite，下次对应engine启动生效；Restore移除两个accepted overlay且保留后续manual command edits及legacy Apple tuning key。whole-game overlay不spill quick/HumanSL；separate SSH whole-game或analysisReuseCurrentEngine在review/apply/launch均unsupported，slow hash后重验，不禁local live。
3. asset/hardware/driver/command变化使recommendation失效并保留defaults；setup discovery/model change/benchmark/import/restore共享busy gate，连已queued动作和late callbacks也不能绕过。close/hide取消worker、invalidate generation；reopen后old success/error/confirmation不得显示或Apply。current saved engine identity不被later discovery retarget。model hashing不在render/event loop，opt-in verify在review/apply/launch，cold verification成本分记。
4. version1的measurement limits保持：engine-only输出不能替app固定budget completion/response/event-loop；whole-game rootVisitsByTurn每position达到budget，Cancel metric是active worker实际process exit，不是JSON termination ACK。3或5独立warm pairs，cold不进runs，有限metrics/scene allowed keys/unsupported schema拒绝；paired speed每pair更快且median至少1.03；三轮CV>10%须五轮，五轮CV>15%拒绝；latency median/max、memory余量阈值按固定schema。sampled total GPU memory不是continuous peak/per-process VRAM，也不证明RAM/heap/RSS无回归；未决RAM regression只能experimental，保留原config，不给unconditional recommendation。
- **验证环境/责任**: source schema/状态/设置fault fixtures；后续真实controlled machine/driver/workload/one-KataGo measurement与native review/apply/restore，样例数值不算证据。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 完整workflow/identity/nonmutation/busy/stale/measurement boundaries均有结果；不能仅threads argv通过。

完整行为/非目标/实际阻塞/证据门：[UD-04-042](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-042), [UD-04-047](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-047), [UD-04-048](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-048).

## B49 — Local same-tree move focus, ordinary cache and display

Current capability: [R13 — 同树重点分析与精确局面确认](../R13_PLAN.md#focus). Historical source: `49-focus.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver the complete local same-tree focus workflow and compatible-engine evidence without waiting for C readboard integration.

### Functional input scope

`B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Use current ordinary direct-local engine incarnation, confirmed position and idle owner for off-UI capability probe before ordinary ponder. Legal pass/probability0/rootInfo and full stop/final fence establish support; version/list_commands do not. Syntax rejection disables focus only, communication uncertainty fails binding rather than caching unsupported. No remote/SSH/WebSocket, dual engine, Match/GMA/webtrial/exclusive/unrestored admission. Add/remove/clear own user set and active gain subset: equal weights total0.5, cumulative current-point visits and fixed source8s strict-point no-progress timer. Completion leaves focus mark but never reactivates; remove/clear safely withdraw gain, request cancel is not withdrawal confirmed. Pause/limit/timeout/error/context retirement never auto-retry. Only reissue ordinary analyze, never clear/loadSGF/replay/change allowavoid or reset ordinary budget. Full response retirement precedes new binding; parser/publication recheck exact root/output/lineage/board/node/slot. First valid complete focus payload replaces whole slot even with lower root count, never grafts trees; before it old cache stays. Slots/maps/ownership remain ordinary, Save/reopen retains ordinary root/order/edge only, not focus intent. Render same ordinary candidate/text/font/order; outline uses same-payload candidate/order0 loss, neutral if absent; completed/Pause outlines and filters honour source054e, toggling outline never restarts. Every source054a/b/c/e/f assertion below is part of this local feature; C owns054d readboard integration and is not a local prerequisite.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Capability probe support/reject/uncertain/late/new Run/Pause cases preserve ordinary stream and pause, no old allow fallback.
- Add/remove/clear/multiple completion/strict8s no-progress/budget/failure match source, blocked points never falsely complete.
- Real compatible writer/parser/publication prove same-tree no-clear/replay and unchanged ordinary budgets; old queued removed update cannot revive.
- Exact root/whole-slot first complete adoption, pre-first cache retention, map/slot isolation and Save/reopen ordinary values are correct; focus intent never restores.
- Outline/filter/completed/Pause/remove/outlineoff/on/missing baseline and migration use ordinary renderer with no synthetic second result.
- Completion records exact Next candidate, binary origin/version/hash/platform and actual compatible same-tree results; source pinned47aadc08518b3e121f22539796c911002f699584 is not a Next pass; final-fence/GPU gaps stay named.
- Local evidence completes without remote credentials/readboard; C consumes B local result for054d only.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B62 — Bound current-game task completion/Cancel/Pause and close/reopen handback

Current capability: [R13 — 自动载入快析与任务交还](../R13_PLAN.md#autoquick). Historical source: `62-handoff.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: Which finite task-window completion/Cancel/Pause/close/reopen semantics are already covered and which immediate timing residual is reachable?

Use H-ANALYSIS original Windows lane-localCancel/handoff evidence; compare Java whole-game window to Next task panel entry/close/reopen reachability. Freeze task/Run/job and one-time handback so Pause is not overridden and old terminal cannot restore new ordinary owner. No new reachable window path can close by precise entry/source comparison; DTO or Start call is not result evidence. Do not wait automatic quick implementation.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Original/new path matrix separates already inherited handback from close/reopen/terminal timing residual.
- Completion/Cancel/Pause preserve original task identity, exactly one return, no old/new owner overwrite.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.
