## B69 — Verify preview input ownership and decide any navigation difference

Current capability: [R13 — 颗粒化显示、悬停、自定义评级与可访问候选](../R13_PLAN.md#display). Historical source: `69-variation-nav.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: Do first/last/longPV preview inputs retain ownership without changing SGF cursor, and is any Java first-step difference actually wanted?

Preserve existing UI03 120ms and current variation/ReviewAutoplay/VariationReplay distinct input contracts. Freeze PV for navigation; hover replacement retires old input/timers. At first/last move wheel/Page belongs preview, never falls through and moves recorded game. Separately name product decision for any source new two-move initial-step versus existing full-step behavior; current accepted default remains until explicit decision. Stop at actual supported interaction matrix and approved/no-change difference, not automatic new shortcut implementation.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- First/last/longPV/hover replacement and ReviewAutoplay/VariationReplay each have supported input owner and unchanged game cursor assertion.
- Any source initial-step difference has explicit retained/no-change decision and approval if non-equivalent; existing delay/default unchanged.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B70 — Accessible candidate-list navigation during live updates

Current capability: [R13 — 颗粒化显示、悬停、自定义评级与可访问候选](../R13_PLAN.md#display). Historical source: `70-candidate-access.md`.

### Capability result

Keep candidate rows readable and navigable across streaming updates and supported layout.

### Functional input scope

`T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Use actual current candidate-list entry under ANA04 current identity; updates preserve meaningful row selection/scroll rather than force top or lock preview. Keyboard/scrollbar are list-owned, no bleed to board or preview when list focused. Narrow height/zoom/shrink clamps legally and keeps complete rows/actions reachable. Absent/stale candidates are not current data and unknown fields stay absent. Java pixel row height is not Next default. This is B consumer improvement; F full a11y/locale closeout consumes it, not a blocker for local implementation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Live refresh permits selected-row browsing with retained valid scroll/selection and honest stale/absent states.
- Keyboard/wheel/scrollbar does not mutate board or force preview; focus state remains accessible.
- Actual narrow/zoom/shrink work-area cases maintain complete usable rows, not Java pixel assumptions; native input evidence recorded.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

<a id="b71"></a>
## B71 — Verify bounded preview calculation/publication and replay retirement

Current capability: [R13 — 颗粒化显示、悬停、自定义评级与可访问候选](../R13_PLAN.md#display). Historical source: `71-preview-async.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: Can supported longPV preview switch/hide/new-game/replay Stop publish stale work or block current input/cancel?

Use frozen input ownership/current supported preview surfaces only, including any independent/floating surface actually reachable; do not add Java window architecture. Measure applicable interaction and cancellation bounded workload and identify worker/publication/replay timer ownership. Preserve compatible visible results until valid replacement, full admitted output and existing hover/nav contract; no invented new worker/time threshold or broad stress run.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Controlled longPV switch/hide/new-game/replayStop fences old worker/timer publication.
- Actual current interaction/cancel scope and measured bounded workload are named, preserving original delay/navigation.
- No reachable Java-style floating surface is recorded as nonapplicable with source entry evidence, not an extra new product.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T05-PREVIEW-ASYNC-CHECK`.

Source intents: `T05-PREVIEW-ASYNC-CHECK`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ANA-04`, `UI-03`, `ANA-13`.

Delta sources: `UD-05-24`.
